pp[81] = function(...)
    if not _G.audioSelectedMusic or _G.audioSelectedMusic == "" then
        return;
    end;
    pcall(function(...)
        a = game;
        f = a.GetService(a, "ReplicatedStorage");
        a = f.FindFirstChild(f, "1Gu1nSound1s", true);
        a.FireServer(a, workspace, _G.audioSelectedMusic, 1);
        return; 
    end);
    task.wait(.2);
    task.spawn(function(...)
        f = game;
        a = f.GetService(f, "ReplicatedStorage").RE;
        r729 = a.FindFirstChild(a, "1Gu1n");
        if not r729 then
            return;
        end;
        E = game;
        b = E.GetService(E, "Players");
        E = {
            b.GetPlayers(b)
        };
        for S, o in pairs(D(f)) do
            b = S;
            r730 = o;
            B = Y[a].Character;
            if B then
                B = Y[a].Character;
                Q = B.FindFirstChild(B, "HumanoidRootPart");
            end;
            if B then
                task.spawn(function(...)
                    pcall(function(...)
                        r731 = Instance.new("Sound");
                        r731.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. _G.audioSelectedMusic;
                        r731.Volume = 1;
                        r731.Parent = Y[o].Character.HumanoidRootPart;
                        a = r731;
                        a.Play(a);
                        a = r731.Ended;
                        a.Connect(a, function(...)
                            a = r731;
                            a.Destroy(a);
                            return; 
                        end);
                        return; 
                    end);
                    return; 
                end);
                task.spawn(function(...)
                    pcall(function(...)
                        local U = {
                            U[1],
                            U[2]
                        };
                        firesignal(Y[U[1]].OnClientEvent, Y[U[2]], Y[U[2]].Character.HumanoidRootPart, Y[U[2]].Character.HumanoidRootPart, Vector3.new(0, -1, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), Y[U[2]].Character.HumanoidRootPart, Y[U[2]].Character.HumanoidRootPart, _G.audioSelectedMusic, _G.audioSelectedMusic, {
                            true
                        }, {
                            25,
                            Vector3.new(.25, .25, 100),
                            BrickColor.new("Bright yellow"),
                            .25,
                            Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic,
                            .25
                        }, true, false);
                        return; 
                    end);
                    return; 
                end);
                task.spawn(function(...)
                    pcall(function(...)
                        local U = {
                            U[1],
                            U[2]
                        };
                        a = Y[U[1]];
                        a.FireServer(a, Y[U[2]].Character.HumanoidRootPart, Y[U[2]].Character.HumanoidRootPart, Vector3.new(0, -1, 0), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0), Y[U[2]].Character.HumanoidRootPart, Y[U[2]].Character.HumanoidRootPart, _G.audioSelectedMusic, _G.audioSelectedMusic, {

pp[84] = function(...)
    q = game.Players.LocalPlayer;
    k = q.Character;
    if k then
        k = q.Character;
        f = k.FindFirstChild(k, "HumanoidRootPart");
    end;
    if k then
        r732 = q.Character.HumanoidRootPart;
        a = q.Character;
        r733 = a.FindFirstChildOfClass(a, "Humanoid");
        r734 = getgenv().locations[getgenv().teleportLocation];
        if r734 then
            pcall(function(...)
                local U = {
                    355,
                    354,
                    356
                };
                if Y[U[1]] then
                    a = Y[U[1]];
                    a.ChangeState(a, Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics);
                    Y[U[1]].WalkSpeed = 0;
                end;
                Y[U[2]].Anchored = true;
                Y[U[2]].CFrame = CFrame.new(Y[U[3]]);
                task.wait(.4);
                Y[U[2]].Anchored = false;
                a = Y[U[1]];
                if a then
                    Y[U[1]].WalkSpeed = 16;
                    a = Y[U[1]];
                    a.ChangeState(a, Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running);
                end;
                return; 
            end);
        end;
    end;
    return; 
end;

pp[77] = function(arg1_2, arg2_2, ...)
    q, k = arg1_2, arg2_2;
    b = {
        q.GetChildren(q)
    };
    for E, Q in pairs(D(f)) do
        o = E;
        if Q.IsA(Q, "BasePart") then
            Q.Material = arg2_2;
        else
            if Q.IsA(Q, "Model") or Q.IsA(Q, "Folder") then
                _G.setMaterialRecursive(Q, arg2_2);
            end;
        end; 
    end;
    return; 
end;
v66, pp[78] = nil, "_G";
pp[78] = Env[pp[78]];
pp[78][pp[79]] = pp[77];
pp[79] = "configureShaderMaterials";
pp[77] = function(arg1, ...)
    q = arg1;
    k = q.FindFirstChild(q, "Model");
    if not k then
        return;
    end;
    if k.FindFirstChild(k, "001_SnowStreet") then
        _G.setMaterialRecursive(k["001_SnowStreet"], Enum.Material.Basalt);
    end;
    if k.FindFirstChild(k, "Street") then
        b = k.Street;
        b = "pairs";
        for E, Q in pairs(b.GetDescendants(b)) do
            o = E;
            if Q.IsA(Q, "BasePart") then
                Q.Material = Enum.Material.Basalt;
            end; 
        end;
    end;
    for b, Q in pairs(k.GetChildren(k)) do
        o = b;
        if Q.IsA(Q, "BasePart") and Q.Name == "Sidewalk" then
            if Q.Material == Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic then
                Q.Material = Enum.Material.Cobblestone;
            end;
        end; 
    end;
    return k; 
end;
